To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>


### [110] [FormalTCS: Benchmarking End-to-End Frontier Formal Theoretical Computer Science Research of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20153)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由专家验证的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在顶会（STOC、FOCS、SODA、COLT 2025-2026）论文基础上的端到端理论计算机科学研究能力。该基准包含175个实例，配有专家验证的Lean形式化证明。评测发现现有模型远不能可靠完成完整研究流程，其中自动形式化是最尖锐的瓶颈（最佳模型仅11.5分，而人类提供形式化语句时的证明得分达28.6）。基于此基准构建的自动化研究框架从64个生成论断中最终仅有6个通过专家评审与证明验证，表明除形式化外，研究品味（research taste）不足是另一大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自动化理论计算机科学研究方面展现出潜力，但现有基准距离真实研究场景相去甚远，无法反映前沿、端到端的研究任务，因此需要一个专家验证、贴近真实论文研究流程的评测基准。

Method: 构建了包含175个实例的基准，实例取自STOC、FOCS、SODA、COLT 2025-2026收录论文，保留论文特有的定义、假设与证明依赖，并提供专家验证的Lean形式化与证明。随后在基准上评测领先LLM，并进一步开发了一个"生成-形式化-过滤-证明"的自动化TCS研究框架。

Result: 现有LLM在完整研究流程上表现不佳：自动形式化是最大瓶颈（最佳模型翻译自然语言论断仅得11.5，而在人类提供形式化论述时的Pass@8为28.6）。自动化框架生成的64个论断中，仅6个最终通过专家评估与证明验证。

Conclusion: 自动形式化和研究品味是当前自主化TCS研究的两个主要障碍，现有模型距离可靠的端到端自动研究仍有很大差距，该基准为后续研究提供了可量化的评测基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [111] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Task-CoEvolve，一种通过自适应验证任务选择来优化 LLM agent harness（智能体执行框架/工具代码）的方法。它利用方差加权采样聚焦于智能体能力边界附近的任务，并在仅评估部分任务时估算全量集合得分，从而在保持与全量搜索相当最终性能的同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少约 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness 优化通过迭代重写 harness 代码并依据验证性能来提升效果，无需更新模型权重。但现有方法每次迭代都在固定验证集上做完整评估，导致成本高昂，尤其当某些任务在 harness 演化后判别性下降时仍被反复评估。因此需要一种能动态选择信息量高的验证任务的机制，同时能从部分评估中可靠估计全量性能。

Method: Task-CoEvolve 让验证任务与 harness 协同演化，解决两个挑战：(1) 信息性任务选择——基于"候选 harness 之间分歧越大任务越有判别力"的观察，依据历史结果采用方差加权采样，将评估集中在智能体能力边界附近的判别性任务上，采样分布随 harness 演化自适应调整；(2) 全量性能估计——利用采样概率对采样任务的得分进行加权修正，从而在每次迭代评估不同子集的情况下仍能进行一致的性能比较。

Result: 在在线文本分类和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上的实验表明，Task-CoEvolve 一致优于固定子集基线，达到了与全量验证集搜索相当的最终性能，同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少了约 80%。

Conclusion: 自适应、任务与 harness 协同演化的验证策略能在显著降低计算成本的同时不损失最终优化效果，为大规模 LLM agent harness 优化提供了高效且可扩展的评估方案。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [112] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IAR（Inject, Align, Recover）三阶段后训练框架，用于文档知识内化（将固定语料库转化为可用的参数化知识），实现免检索的问答。与常规持续预训练不同，Inject 阶段将源文档转化为续写、改写和基于指令的条件重建目标；Align 阶段用纯答案的 QA 监督适配模型；Recover 阶段将领域适配模型与基础指令模型合并以恢复通用能力。在 CC 和 CCI 语料及 Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM 模型族上，IAR 改善了面向免检索文档内化的领域-通用性能前沿；主对比中 IAR 在 8 个数据-模型设置里的 7 个中全面优于 Vanilla SFT，领域 QA 准确率平均提升 3.6 个百分点，通用性能（IFEval、MMLU、MSBench）平均提升 12.1 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理时无法检索源文档时，常常无法回答涉及有界文档集合的问题。作者将该场景定义为"文档知识内化"（document knowledge internalization），即把固定语料转化为可用的参数化知识，以实现免检索问答；现有常规持续预训练方法未能在领域内化与通用能力保持之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出 IAR 三阶段后训练框架：(1) Inject——将源文档转换为三种目标（续写 continuation、改写 rewrite、指令条件重建 instruction-conditioned reconstruction）实现结构化知识注入，区别于常规持续预训练；(2) Align——使用答案式 QA 监督适配已注入的模型，对齐问答行为；(3) Recover——将领域适配模型与基础指令模型进行模型合并，恢复通用能力。

Result: 在 Common Corpus（CC）和 CCI 语料上，覆盖 Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM 多个模型族。主对比中，IAR 在 8 个数据集-模型设置中的 7 个中，于全部 4 项指标上优于 Vanilla SFT：领域 QA 准确率平均提升 3.6 个百分点，通用性能（IFEval、MMLU、MSBench）均值提升 12.1 个百分点。扩展的 CC 基线中，LoRA 和 FAPM 可在个别通用指标上占优，但在同时达到领先或接近领先领域内化水平的方法中，IAR 仍保持最强的通用性能画像。

Conclusion: IAR 通过将知识注入、QA 行为对齐与通用能力恢复解耦，为免检索文档知识内化提供了有效的三阶段后训练方案，在领域内化与通用能力之间实现了更优的权衡（更好的"领域-通用前沿"）。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [113] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法，从自然主义的计算机使用轨迹（截图、鼠标/键盘操作）中自主发现潜在任务、解耦并发活动，并为每个任务归纳出由层级目标模型与程序控制流模型组成的结构化任务模型。在受控实验中，TMI对交错任务的分组与人工标注一致性达0.974，重构74.9%的执行步骤，远超最强工作流归纳基线；其派生的技能使留出任务准确率相对提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体进入真实工作场景后，需要学习任务实际如何执行，组织也需要审计和重用这类知识。自然记录的低层事件轨迹是构建符号化、可审计、可重用任务模型的宝贵资源，但真实工作是多线程、目标交错的，且现有方法假设任务已知或工作流单一，只能生成步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型。

Method: TMI包含两个核心步骤：(i) 在无约束的轨迹中发现潜在任务，将并发活动解耦；(ii) 对每个潜在任务归纳任务模型——该模型将递归目标分解的层级目标模型与组织执行的控制流程序模型配对。整体把非结构化低层事件序列转化为可分组的潜在任务及其结构化流程模型。

Result: 内在评估：在受控的人类与智能体轨迹上，交错任务分组与真实标记的一致性达0.974，重构74.9%的已观测执行步骤，优于最强工作流归纳基线。外在评估：由TMI任务模型派生的技能使留出任务准确率较最强基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: TMI证明可以从无约束、多线程的真实计算轨迹中自动归纳出结构化、可审计、可复用的任务模型，显著优于现有工作流归纳基线，为计算机使用智能体学习和组织知识审计重用提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [114] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向患者的医学报告解读（PMRI）这一新的开放式多模态生成任务，要求模型基于用户查询和对话历史，以准确且通俗的语言解释医学报告。为解决"医学事实性"与"患者沟通"双重目标难以在传统监督微调和整体式强化学习下联合优化的问题，作者提出G-CARL框架，结合多源检索的原子声明验证与上下文感知、实例特定的加权清单，提供事实性、需求满足和表达质量的结构化监督；并构建了真实世界基准MMedReport及临床医生设计的三维评估协议。实验表明G-CARL在总体质量、声明级精度和清单召回率上均优于现有后训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个性化解读需求日益增长，这同时要求"基于证据的医学事实性"和"依赖上下文的患者沟通"。然而现有医学视觉-语言任务未能充分涵盖这两个要求，且这两个目标在可验证性上截然不同却又紧密耦合，使得在传统监督微调和整体式强化学习范式下难以联合优化。

Method: 1) 定义PMRI任务：基于用户查询和对话历史，对医学报告进行准确且通俗的开放式多模态生成；2) 提出G-CARL框架：将多源检索用于原子声明验证（grounded factuality），结合上下文感知、实例特定的加权检查清单（weighted checklists）用于响应覆盖度，提供面向事实性、用户需求满足和表达质量的结构化监督，同时不限制响应多样性；3) 构建真实世界基准MMedReport，并设计了由临床医生制定的三维评估协议。

Result: 大量实验表明，G-CARL在总体质量、声明级精度（claim-level precision）和清单召回率（checklist recall）上持续优于现有后训练基线；临床医生的成对偏好评估进一步确认，G-CARL生成的解读更准确、更贴合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL有效弥合了医学事实性与患者沟通之间的鸿沟，为医学报告解读这一兼具可验证性和上下文依赖性的生成任务提供了可行的训练范式与评估基准，有望推动面向患者的多模态医学文本生成研究。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [115] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有LLM“遗忘”（unlearning）方法与基准的不足，提出遗忘应在“概念”层面而非孤立事实层面进行，并引入“双重用途概念”（dual-use concepts）的概念，构建基准ConceptGuard，其遗忘集与保留集在概念使用上互补，评估对意图敏感并以最大化上下文分离为目标。实验表明现有遗忘技术在该设置下表现不佳，存在强遗忘-保留权衡、上下文灵敏度提升有限以及概念级控制不一致等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越需要选择性移除有害或敏感知识（即遗忘），但现有方法和基准无法完整评估该能力。现有方法依赖由独立事实构成的互不相交的遗忘集与保留集，并用简单直接的问答回忆来衡量成功。这种框架未能捕捉遗忘的关键要求：既能消除有害行为，同时保留良性有益的知识。因此需要从实用视角、在概念层面评估遗忘技术。

Method: 提出双重用途概念（可同时在有害与良性情境使用的概念），并基于此构建基准ConceptGuard。该基准中遗忘集与保留集在概念使用上显式互补，使遗忘能在概念（而非稀疏事实）层面被探索和衡量；评估是意图敏感的，以最大化上下文分离为目标，从而促进更安全的行为。

Result: 实验表明当前遗忘技术在该设置下表现不佳：上下文分离能力弱，ROUGE与概念级指标表现差。结果揭示了强遗忘-保留效用权衡、上下文敏感性提升有限，以及各方法在概念级控制上的不一致性。

Conclusion: 现有遗忘方法难以满足概念级、上下文分离的实际安全需求；研究结果为更贴近真实世界安全要求的遗忘方法提供了思路。数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督式机器学习的新型直升机起飞重量估计模型，利用空客全球在役机队的大量数据集进行训练。研究遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准，构建了一套学习保证流程，包括机器学习需求、模型描述及基于长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络的实现，并在实现上完成需求验证。在传统航电计算机上演示表明，该实现可部署于机载目标平台，服务于机载告警等关键功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是飞行安全的关键参数，对机载告警等安全关键功能至关重要。传统重量估计方法可能依赖人工输入或简化模型，精度和自动化程度有限。因此需要借助机器学习从海量在役数据中实现更准确、可自动化的重量估计，同时需满足航空领域严格的安全性认证要求。

Method: 采用监督式机器学习，以空客全球在役机队的海量数据集为训练基础；使用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络构建重量估计器。整个开发过程遵循学习保证流程，与EASA机器学习应用概念文件和Eurocae ED-324标准对齐，并定义了一套机器学习需求（MLR）与机器学习模型描述（MLMD），最终对实现进行需求验证。

Result: 模型已在传统航电计算机（legacy avionics computers）上完成演示与验证，证明所开发的机器学习重量估计模型可成功部署于机载目标硬件，用于机载告警等关键功能。

Conclusion: 该LSTM重量估计模型结合规范化的学习保证流程，既满足航空适航认证框架（EASA/ED-324）的要求，又在传统机载计算平台上具备实际可用性，为机器学习在航空安全关键功能上的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [117] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量——三角模糊重缩放距离（d_TR），该度量将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到距离计算中，在比较模糊数时自动完成归一化，适用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性数据。作者形式化证明了d_TR满足度量性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并展示了其有界、尺度不变和原点不变等特性。结合维度权重向量，d_TR适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统决策常涉及不精确或不确定信息，通常用模糊集特别是三角模糊数（TFN）表示。许多模糊方法需要度量TFN之间的距离，但现有距离测度大多假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需预先进行归一化。这种预归一化步骤增加了复杂度，作者旨在设计一种无需单独预处理、将归一化内置于距离计算本身的度量。

Method: 作者提出d_TR度量，其核心创新在于将线性重缩放（LRE）直接融入距离计算过程，从而在模糊数比较中自动完成归一化。作者形式化证明了d_TR满足度量公理（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并进一步论证了其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。度量还引入了维度权重向量以支持对特定维度的优先级设置。

Result: d_TR被正式证明是满足全部度量性质的可行度量，同时具备有界性、尺度不变性与原点不变性等理想特性，并与加权向量兼容，能够有效处理异构模糊数据。

Conclusion: d_TR凭借其内置归一化能力与理想的数学性质，适用于涉及异构模糊数据的各类应用场景，包括合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助，为模糊距离度量提供了更便捷、鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [118] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Holtercare-23K，一个大规模多模态动态心电图数据集，包含来自 788 份临床 Holter 记录的 22,980 组问答对，并引入信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；同时构建 Holtercare-Bench 基准，用以评测 MLLM 在时间定位、临床诊断与全局总结上的能力。零样本评测显示主流 MLLM 在超长病理序列处理上存在明显差距，而微调后性能显著提升，为长期医学 MLLM 提供了基础基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在医学应用中表现优异，但多偏向静态图像或短时信号；在动态心电图（ECG）这一关键领域，模型因缺乏高质量数据集与基准，难以进行复杂时间推理和诊断报告生成。作者旨在填补动态心电长序列数据的空白。

Method: (i) 构建 Holtercare-23K：基于 788 份临床 Holter 记录生成 22,980 组问答对，采用新颖的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；(ii) 在此基础上推出 Holtercare-Bench 多模态基准，从时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度评估模型；并通过对代表性 MLLM 进行零样本评测与微调来验证。

Result: 零样本评测表明，领先 MLLM 在处理超长病理心电序列时存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后获得了大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前 MLLM 在电生理学（动态心电）长序列理解上的局限，为长期医学 MLLM 提供了基础性基准与数据集，项目已开源。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用。通过特征选择、角度/密集角度特征映射及多种纠缠策略将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间，计算基于保真度的量子核并与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，与原始特征的SVM基线比较。结果表明量子核模型在片段组学数据集上多个20特征配置显著提升了AUC，在甲基化数据集上虽未超过经典SVM但部分模型改进了特异性；特征数从20增至40未稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量胸部CT肺癌筛查能降低死亡率，但受制于依从性和管理挑战；cfDNA血液生物标志物是补充手段，但由于肺癌异质性以及高维、非线性分子信号，早期检测仍很困难。作者旨在探索量子核方法能否更好捕捉cfDNA片段组学和甲基化中的非线性结构以提升肺癌检测性能。

Method: 使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化两个数据集，先进行特征选择得到20特征和40特征子集；采用角度（angle）和密集角度（dense-angle）特征映射搭配多种纠缠策略将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间；基于精确态矢模拟计算保真度量子核，集成预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归；与在原始特征上训练的经典SVM基线对比，并进行多次留出（held-out）评估。

Result: 在多次留出评估中，量子核模型在两个数据集上都达到有竞争力的性能。片段组学上，多个20特征配置相比经典SVM基线提升了AUC，表明能有效捕获cfDNA片段化的非线性结构；甲基化上，经典SVM AUC最高，但选定的量子模型仍具竞争力且部分提升了特异性；特征数从20增至40未带来一致性提升，反而常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 编码与纠缠设计可系统性地影响分类性能；总体结果支持量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景途径，但目前量子模型性能仅与经典SVM相当或部分超越，后续需更多优化以应对复杂生物信号。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [120] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用目标数据集构建简单分类器的方法来增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）：以现有零样本不确定性分数和相似查询的正确答案作为特征，预测LLM回答的正确性，从而在部署前的数据集评估中持续优于现有UQ分数，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM安全部署离不开不确定性量化，然而现有的UQ方法（如口头化置信度、多次采样等）通常是零样本的，无需标注数据即可产出分数，但在实际应用中部署前仍需在目标数据集上评估其性能；作者认为可以充分利用这一评估过程来改进不确定性分数。

Method: 作者利用部署前可获取的目标数据集，将现有UQ分数和相似查询的答案正确性作为特征，训练简单的分类器来预测LLM当前回答的正确性，作为原有零样本UQ分数的增强；该方法不依赖额外生成，计算开销最小。

Result: 实验表明，利用目标数据集构建的简单分类器能够一致地（consistently）超越现有零样本UQ分数，且只带来极小的计算开销，是一种廉价而直接的增强。

Conclusion: 在部署前的数据集评估环节中加入基于特征的简单分类器，可以显著提升LLM不确定性量化的性能，为实际落地提供低成本、易实现的增强方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [121] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层成像"（mechanistic tomography）这一统一框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积、子集干预等机制可解释性测量方法形式化为 y = Ax + w 的共享测量结构，并给出"从低成本测量出发、在留出干预上检验、校准失配、必要时扩展测量族"的实用流程。在二-HMM控制任务上验证了观测器误差与控制误差的关系；在GPT-2-small上的IOI任务和Qwen-2.5-7B的拒绝任务中展示了该流程如何判断是否需要引入高阶（成对）测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性需要恢复模型不直接暴露的量（表征状态、组件效应、交互作用及干预响应），但现有方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积、子集干预）在访问假设、目标量和测量含义上各不相同，缺乏统一的共享测量结构，也难以系统地在不同干预复杂度和成本之间做选择与校准。

Method: 将各种测量统一为 y = Ax + w 的线性测量模型：A描述所施加的干预族，x为待恢复的目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配。由此形成实用流程：(1) 先采用最小成本测量；(2) 在目标尺度的留出干预上测试；(3) 校准简单失配；(4) 若存在结构化残差则扩展测量族（如提升测量、Hessian-向量积以捕捉一阶映射遗漏的交互）。并以控制任务作为判别性验证场景（估计引导干预时估计器充当观测器）。

Result: 在二-HMM模型上：控制误差随观测器误差上升，而目标改善可能掩盖干扰状态（nuisance-state）的移动；仅前向访问下，稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改善局部归因映射。提升测量与Hessian-向量积可恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互，Tracr表明所需测量族取决于基的选择。在GPT-2-small IOI上，Name Mover与Negative Name Mover之间的交互是三个测试跨组对中最大的留出预测项；在Qwen-2.5-7B上，有限校准使加性拒绝响应映射足够好，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层成像为测量类方法提供了统一语言和实用决策流程，使研究者能依据留出干预误差系统性地决定何时扩展测量族。但所需测量族高度依赖基与任务（Tracr表明基依赖；Qwen上无需高阶项），说明不存在放之四海皆准的测量方案，需用留出验证来指导选择；同时控制场景警示：仅看目标指标改善可能隐藏无关状态的偏离。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [122] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对基于模板（中间层抽象状态复用）的神经网络鲁棒性不完整验证加速技术进行了系统性研究，提出了“联合稳定神经元”新指标来解释其效果差异，并设计了 FastCert 技术在层间自动分配模板以提升加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络被应用于许多关键领域，鲁棒性验证愈发重要。已有研究显示证明共享/模板复用可以加速不完整验证，但模板加速在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的稳健性仍不明确，亟需系统研究其有效性与局限性。

Method: 首先对模板复用的覆盖面进行系统研究，发现模板包含率在不同场景下变化很大；提出“联合稳定神经元”这一新指标解释这种变化，指出某些场景下模板技术几乎不可能带来加速；随后提出 FastCert，通过自动将模板分布到神经网络各层来增强性能影响，并在模板不太可能产生加速时完全放弃模板。

Result: 在大量基于覆盖设计的 L0 验证任务上，FastCert 与现有基于模板的复用技术相比取得了平均 1.13 倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速的有效性因场景而异，联合稳定神经元指标可预测模板加速的潜力；FastCert 通过自动分配或跳过模板能够提高验证加速效果，为模板复用技术的应用提供了更可靠的选择。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [123] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)，一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架，利用纵向弥散张量成像（DTI）连续估计阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度。DCP将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，联合整合纵向观测与弱临床监督，并由此推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)来量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及其不确定性。在ADNI队列上的实验表明DCP优于代表性疾病进展方法，且DCS能准确刻画疾病严重程度、具有强临床相关性、保持纵向演化信息并预测未来疾病转化，为AD进展的连续评估提供了超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的定量影像学表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD本质上是一个连续的生物过程，而现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于从横断面影像进行离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续进展特性。这一矛盾催生了对能够在时间维度上连续刻画疾病严重程度的建模方法的需求。

Method: DCP是一个纵向贝叶斯学习框架，其核心是将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，通过联合整合纵向观测数据与弱临床监督进行推断。该框架利用贝叶斯机制建模不确定性，并在此概率潜变量基础上定义DCS，以量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及其伴随的不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验显示：DCP在疾病进展任务上持续优于代表性方法；综合验证分析表明DCS能够准确刻画疾病严重程度、展现强临床相关性、保留纵向疾病演化特征，并能预测未来的疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了超越传统诊断标签与临床评分的定量影像学表征，实现了对AD进展的连续化、带不确定性的评估，为疾病的连续监测与预后预测提供了新的表示范式。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [124] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，用于预测网络安全披露等外部冲击事件发生后短期金融异常损失。该框架结合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据，通过事件融合模块与动态对比学习目标应对事件罕见、稀疏监督和背景噪声等挑战，并在新构建的真实数据集 SECURE 上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全泄露披露等冲击会通过网络传播并突然扰乱金融时间序列，造成重大异常损失，但此类事件以离散记录发生、后果却在连续市场动态中展开。现有时间序列预测模型主要关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件；同时高影响事件稀疏且夹杂市场噪声，使得事件条件下的影响预测（估计披露后的短期异常损失，而非重建完整轨迹）极具挑战。

Method: EventTime 是一个多分辨率框架，融合了三个互补的信息来源：长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据。其核心包括一个事件融合模块，将时间序列表征与事件属性耦合，以识别相关的近期市场模式；为缓解稀疏监督，引入动态对比学习目标，在训练期间构造事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对。此外，作者构建了 SECURE 数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列以及结构化/LLM 生成的语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明，EventTime 在估算事件后金融损失方面持续优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其表征对事件更敏感、对不完整元数据更具鲁棒性，并且对网络安全披露后的短期市场影响估计更具可解释性。

Conclusion: EventTime 有效弥合了离散事件记录与连续市场动态之间的鸿沟，为事件条件下短期金融影响预测提供了一套可行框架，并为网络安全披露对应的市场反应建模提供了更好的表征、鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [125] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLM-Detector，一种利用大语言模型（LLM）上下文学习能力进行表格数据异常检测的框架。它无需微调 LLM 或训练神经网络，而是将正常训练数据转化为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词以生成代码评分引擎，对每个测试样本计算统计偏差、结构不一致和基于密度的异常分数。在 24 个表格数据集上与 15 个 SOTA 基线对比，在混合类型和连续型设定下均取得一致的性能提升，同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本往往表现为跨特征依赖的违反，而非简单的单变量边缘偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而基于 LLM 的方法通常需要对正常样本进行微调或生成合成异常，开销大且泛化有限。因此作者希望利用 LLM 的上下文学习能力，直接从结构化正常状态知识中推导异常检测逻辑，避免训练成本。

Method: LLM-Detector 将正常训练数据转换为三类结构化知识：统计摘要（statistical summaries）、因果依赖（causal dependencies）和蒸馏原型（distilled prototypes），并将这些知识组织为提示词（prompt）用于代码生成。生成的评分引擎对每个测试样本综合评估统计偏差（statistical deviation）、结构不一致（structural inconsistency）和基于密度的异常性（density-based abnormality），最终计算统一异常分数。该设计全程无需 LLM 微调或神经网络训练。

Result: 在 24 个表格数据集上与 15 个 SOTA 基线对比，LLM-Detector 在混合类型（mixed-type）和纯连续（continuous-only）设定下均取得一致的性能提升，同时消除了 LLM 微调与神经网络训练需求，显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector 表明，通过提示词驱动的结构化评分合成，LLM 的上下文学习能力可以替代传统训练流程实现有效的表格异常检测。该框架在保证检测精度的前提下大幅减少计算开销，为真实世界表格系统中的异常检测提供了实用且可部署的方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [126] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过3,933次实验运行的受控实证研究，发现在概念漂移下最关键的决策不是选择何种重训练策略，而是部署的模型是否进行增量学习：采用增量更新时，重训练策略与不重训练基线无实际显著差异；无增量更新时，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于基于误差阈值和ADWIN的反应式策略，而反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保有优势。论文还揭示了反应式策略的系统性失败模式及延迟-预算排队交互作用可使有效重训练预算减半，并开源了完整模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但从业者对何时应重训练缺乏原则性指导。重训练成本高昂、预算有限，且模型重训练并非即时生效——训练与部署延迟会使陈旧模型在数据持续移动时继续提供预测，这些现实约束在以往研究中未被显式建模。为弥补这一空白，论文建立一个将重训练预算和训练+部署延迟显式化的统一系统模型，对不同策略进行受控对比。

Method: 论文开展受控实证研究，在统一系统模型下将三种实用的模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发重训练）与不重训练基线比较。实验覆盖3,933次运行，涵盖三种漂移模式（突变/渐变/循环）、三个预算水平、至多五级延迟、三个数据集和两种学习模式（增量与非增量），并进行54组配对比较以评估统计与实践显著性。

Result: 核心发现：对采用线性在线学习器、具备即时效标签的增量更新模型，54组配对比较中没有任何策略与不重训练基线产生实际显著差异（即使极端延迟下也是如此）；对非增量模型，策略选择可使漂移后准确率产生15-55个百分点的差异，其中周期性重训练在突变与渐变漂移下显著优于两种反应式策略，反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保持优势。此外论文记录了反应式策略的系统性失败模式，并发现延迟-预算排队交互会使有效重训练预算静默减半。

Conclusion: 研究结论是最具决定性影响的系统设计决策是模型是否增量学习，而非重训练策略本身；在非增量场景下，简单的周期性重训练通常是更稳健的选择。论文公开了完整模拟器、数据集流水线及逐次运行产物，以保证研究可复现。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [127] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeltaMomentum，一种方向感知的动量更新规则：利用线性层梯度的键值结构（输入为键、输出侧误差为值），用规范 delta 规则更新动量缓冲，使得每个方向以与其出现频率匹配的速率被遗忘。它是任何优化器动量缓冲的即插即用替代品，在 FineWeb-Edu 预训练中显著减少达到同等验证损失的步数，且训练时诊断证实了机制预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器的动量都采用梯度指数移动平均（EMA），以单一固定速率遗忘所有方向。但深度网络在训练中看到的输入具有高度各向异性：少数方向被频繁查询，多数方向很少出现。近期方法通过在动量缓冲外增加额外处理来应对这种各向异性，但动量更新本身未变。作者希望把方向感知直接构建进动量更新规则中。

Method: DeltaMomentum 的核心观察是：线性层的梯度可分解为充当"键"的输入和充当"值"的输出侧误差。利用这一键值结构，动量缓冲通过规范 delta 规则更新，使每个方向以其出现频率决定的速率被遗忘。它可作为任意优化器动量缓冲的即插即用替代品；在 μP 下系数可跨宽度迁移，额外计算仅为门控 MLP 块线性成本的 22.2%–25.0%，且无持久内存开销。

Result: 作者证明了它是合法动量、无需矩阵求逆即可施加输入侧曲率校正，且在固定/漂移最优点下比 EMA 更快清除过时方向。在 FineWeb-Edu 预训练中，DeltaAdamW 在三颗种子上以最多 46.39±4.32%（67M）和 22.12±0.80%（370M）更少的步数达到 AdamW 的验证损失，增益在 Chinchilla 最优预算下的 1B 规模仍存在；同协议下调优的 Muon 基线劣于 DeltaAdamW，增益在 SGD、ResNet-18 和 CIFAR-10 上的 ViT-Tiny 上同样成立。

Conclusion: 训练时诊断确认了预测机制：DeltaMomentum 带来更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向，证明将方向感知纳入动量更新本身是应对训练输入各向异性的有效途径。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种操作性能力层级（operational capability hierarchy）和 Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate（DBOSC），用于检验多模态世界模型中不同传感器学到的紧凑表征是否承载相同的"可执行含义"、以及在新的动作组合（held-out 程序）下该含义是否保持不变。在 Cluster Haptic 数据上，音频与加速度表征对同一未见表面在响应空间中比错误表面配对近 4.5 倍，且 19 个留出表面全部成立；在受控弹塑性系统上，冻结执行器在预注册预算下无法推进任意精确图表坐标通过留出程序（门槛拒绝），但收敛预算下同一 rank-three 图表可执行程序（oracle NMSE 0.18），融合优于单一模态，16 项注册检查中 14 项通过。作者还证明"通过门槛"取决于执行器而非图表（整程序发射型执行器比实体盲预测器差 38 倍），并给出非可辨识性结果说明仅靠压缩与融合无法确定未见组合律，从而把属性访问、响应替换、融合封闭性和有序执行区分为各自可独立检验的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探针无法回答两个关键问题：不同传感器（如音频与加速度）的表征是否具有相同的可执行语义，以及这种语义在遇到训练中未出现的新动作组合时是否仍然成立。世界模型把紧凑多模态表征当作感知与物理交互之间的接口，但缺乏一套能够区分"能访问属性"、"能替换响应"、"融合闭环"和"有序执行"等不同能力层次的检验标准，因此需要一个新的操作性能力层级和对应的验证协议。

Method: 作者提出 DBOSC（Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate）：让独立训练的模态编译器在训练面板之外的证据上互换地进入同一个冻结的响应图表（frozen response chart），以检验跨模态可替换性。在 Cluster Haptic 上用响应空间距离度量验证同表面跨模态表征的接近度，并解封留出响应检验物理预测优于总体图表；在受控弹塑性系统中（模态具有互补盲区）测试有序执行，比较预注册预算与收敛预算下冻结执行器对留出程序的执行能力，并用 16 个注册检查、融合信息矩阵的对角限制分析以及整程序发射型执行器的对照实验来定位失败原因，最后给出压缩/融合无法确定未见组合律的非可辨识性结果。

Result: Cluster Haptic 上音频与加速度表征对同一未见表面比错误表面配对接近 4.5 倍，19 个留出表面全部保持该差距；解封验证显示每个分支的物理预测都好于总体图表。在弹塑性系统上，预注册预算下门槛被拒绝（冻结执行器无法推进图表坐标通过留出程序）；收敛预算下同一 rank-three 图表能执行程序（oracle NMSE 0.18），融合优于任一模态，16 项注册检查通过 14 项，2 项失败源于融合信息矩阵的对角限制与全矩阵表现相当；整程序发射型执行器比实体盲预测器差 38 倍，证明门槛通过取决于执行器而非图表。

Conclusion: 多模态表征的"可执行等价性"必须通过独立于训练面板的替代性检验才能确立；能否执行未见组合取决于执行器本身的动力学能力，而非表征图表或压缩/融合过程。作者将属性访问、响应替换、融合封闭性与有序执行确定为四个层面分开、可各自独立测试的能力，并指出压缩与融合在理论上无法单独确定未见组合律（非可辨识性），为未来世界模型的能力认证提供了分层评测框架。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [129] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过核典型对齐、核漂移、谱集中度和训练损失等经验神经正切核（NTK）诊断，系统比较了紧凑型混合量子预测模型与结构对齐的经典基线模型的学习动力学。在平稳谐波混合与非平稳啁啾信号基准上，两类模型表现出截然不同的优化几何（经典模型早期目标对齐更强，混合模型核谱更分散、核漂移更小），但最终泛化性能相当。混合模型仅用125个可训练参数（经典为281个），且在18种频率条件中的15种更早达到验证集最优检查点。结果表明：相近的泛化能力可源自不同的学习轨迹，单一NTK指标不能单调预测验证收敛，架构相关学习行为被端点精度指标所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习研究多以最终精度或准确率对比来论证量子优势，忽略了训练过程本身的学习动力学差异。作者旨在揭示混合量子模型与经典模型在优化轨迹、核几何上的结构性差异，说明端点精度相同并不代表学习过程相同，从而为理解量子架构的潜在价值提供更细致的动力学视角。

Method: 采用结构对齐的混合量子模型与经典基线模型，在平稳谐波混合和非平稳啁啾两类基准上控制谱复杂度和数据可用性。通过经验NTK分析框架，计算核-目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度及训练损失四项指标刻画学习动力学；并设置两类对照实验：傅里叶增强的经典基线（检验是否能复现混合模型行为）以及受控的数据重上传消融（检验重复编码对优化与核几何的影响）。

Result: 经典模型具有更强的早期核-目标对齐；混合模型核谱集中度更低、核漂移更小。尽管优化几何截然不同，两种架构在各类条件下的留出性能相近。混合模型参数更少（125 vs 281），并在18种频率条件的15种中更早达到验证选择的检查点。傅里叶增强经典基线无法复现混合模型的训练行为；数据重上传消融证实重复编码会系统性改变优化与核几何。此外，单个NTK诊断指标与验证收敛之间不存在单调预测关系。

Conclusion: 可比的泛化性能可以从显著不同的学习轨迹中涌现，端点精度指标掩盖了架构依赖的学习行为，因此不应以最终准确率作为评判量子模型价值的唯一依据。研究不主张普遍的量子优势，而是强调NTK动力学诊断可作为揭示量子-经典学习机制差异的补充工具，为未来量子模型设计提供动力学层面的指导。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [130] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 4MAS（4 Module Awake/Sleep）宏观架构，借鉴生物记忆巩固机制——经验回放、REM 睡眠和大脑双半球（双侧性）——来解决机器学习中的持续学习难题。该架构包含两个不对称的"半球"，每个半球拥有独立的长期与短期记忆模块，并在增量学习任务之间插入睡眠阶段以促进记忆巩固。在 Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST 和 Split-CIFAR-100 上分别取得 98.3%、84.9% 和 29.29% 的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习的持续学习（continual learning）存在根本性痛点：算法必须在保留旧知识与适应新数据之间权衡，常发生灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）；若缺乏持续学习能力，则需用全量数据重训，这在原始数据因存储、计算/资金成本或隐私限制而不可用时不可行。相比之下，生物（动物）能够持续学习且不发生灾难性遗忘。作者试图通过建模与记忆巩固相关的神经组件和状态，构建一个高级框架来解释动物如何学习与保存知识。

Method: 论文聚焦三个生物学概念：经验回放（experience replay）、REM 睡眠和双侧性（bilaterality，即大脑双半球不对称结构）。在此基础上提出 4MAS 宏观架构：每个半球拥有各自独立的长时与短时记忆机制，形成 4 个模块；在递增学习任务之间引入"睡眠"阶段，以促进记忆巩固。该架构展示了机器学习模型如何从不对称的半球+长短时记忆组织中受益。

Result: 4MAS 在三个基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果：Split-MNIST 准确率 98.3%，Split-Fashion-MNIST 准确率 84.9%，Split-CIFAR-100 准确率 29.29%。

Conclusion: 作者认为，借鉴生物系统的"清醒/睡眠"循环与双侧大脑结构，可以为机器学习持续学习提供可行的宏观设计范式；实验结果表明该架构能有效缓解灾难性遗忘并实现持续学习，同时仍保持与现有方法相当的性能。该工作为后续将更多神经生物学机制引入持续学习系统提供了框架性基础。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [131] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MeanFlow-Transfer（MF-T）与 Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow（CAMF），解决“在新领域上以有限数据快速训练生成器”的两大难题：将异构预训练模型（DiT/SiT/JiT/iMF 等不同参数化）统一映射到共享的平均速度表示以同时完成领域适应与少步加速；并扩展连续对抗流程，将对抗细化从瞬时速度推广到 MeanFlow 的有限区间平均速度。实验表明，在四个 ImageNet 源模型适配五个目标域时，MF-T+CAMF 在高达 125 倍更少的 NFEs 下达到或超过微调教师模型的 FID/FDD，且 CAMF 平均将 MF-T 的少步 FID 提升 29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两方面动机：(1) 将预训练扩散/流模型适配到新域时，其昂贵的多步采样问题未被解决，且现有加速方法耦合于源参数化（ε、x、v、u），异构预训练模型缺乏统一的加速目标；(2) 对抗细化虽已被证明对少步生成质量有效，但仅针对瞬时速度流构造，无法用于 MeanFlow 模型所预测的有限区间平均速度。

Method: 提出两大组件：MeanFlow-Transfer（MF-T）将异构源模型输出映射为共享的速度表示，以此为 MF 生成器初始化（利用源权重）并在目标域优化 MF 目标函数，从而将适应与加速统一到单个训练循环；Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow（CAMF）作为后训练阶段，把连续对抗流模型从瞬时速度推广到 MF 的有限区间平均速度，通过学习势函数在真实与预测区间端点间的变化对比来恢复被 MF 回归平均化的细节，且在区间趋近于零时退化为瞬时准则。

Result: 将四个基于 ImageNet 的源模型（DiT(ε)、SiT(v)、JiT(x)、iMF(u)）适配到五个目标域：MF-T+CAMF 在高达 125 倍更少的神经函数求值次数（NFEs）下，在 FID 与 FDD 指标上达到或超过微调教师模型；CAMF 平均将 MF-T 的少步 FID 提升 29%。

Conclusion: MF-T 提供了一条统一异构预训练模型进行领域适应与加速的通用路径，CAMF 则补全了对抗细化在有限区间平均速度上的缺环，二者结合可在极低 NFEs 下实现高质量少步生成，具备广泛的跨架构适用性。

Abstract: Training fast generators on new domains with limited data remains challenging for two reasons. First, adapting a pretrained diffusion or flow model to a new domain leaves its costly multi-step sampling unaddressed, and existing acceleration methods are tied to the source parameterization--$ε$, $x$, $v$, or $u$--leaving heterogeneous pretrained models with no common acceleration target. Second, while adversarial refinement is proven effective for few-step quality, it is formulated only for instantaneous-velocity flows, not for the finite-interval average velocities that MeanFlow (MF) models predict. We address both problems. We propose MeanFlow-Transfer, which maps heterogeneous source outputs into a shared velocity representation, uses it to initialize an MF generator from the source weights, and optimizes an MF objective on the target domain. This unifies adaptation and acceleration in a single training loop across a broad range of pretrained models. We then introduce Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow, a post-training stage that extends continuous adversarial flow models from instantaneous velocities to MF's finite-interval average velocities. CAMF contrasts changes in a learned potential between real and predicted interval endpoints, recovering fine detail that MF regression averages away, and reduces to the instantaneous criterion in the vanishing-interval limit. Adapting four ImageNet-based source models--DiT ($ε$), SiT ($v$), JiT ($x$), iMF ($u$)--to five target domains, MF-T with CAMF matches or exceeds the fine-tuned teacher in FID and FDD at up to $125\times$ fewer Neural Function Evaluations (NFEs), while CAMF improves MF-T's few-step FID by $29\%$ on average.

</details>


### [132] [DraftFM: A FoundationModel for Day-Zero Drafting in Magic: The Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19568)
*Brian Ward*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DraftFM，一个仅依据公开卡牌记录（结构化特征+固定文本嵌入）的离散选择策略，可在全新未发布系列（day-zero）上直接预测选秀，无需任何历史使用数据或卡牌身份信息。在三个完全未参与训练的扩展上达到50.8%/60.4%/56.7%的top-1人类选牌契合度，并成功生成未发布系列“The Hobbit”的卡牌排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新万智牌扩展发布时完整卡牌列表已经公开，但该扩展的选秀日志尚不存在，无法直接训练传统的监督式选秀模型。需要一种能从公开卡牌特征出发、对未见卡牌同样打分的策略，以应对“day-zero”场景。

Method: DraftFM将选秀建模为离散选择问题：对当前包中每张卡，仅使用其775维冻结表示（结构化特征+固定文本嵌入），并联合已选卡池与草稿状态进行精确评分。模型不包含卡牌身份、系列身份或使用统计，因此新卡与熟悉卡使用同一评分机制。用一个160万参数网络在29个扩展的1.49亿人类选秀上进行训练和评估。

Result: 在完全保留的三个扩展上，top-1预测与人类选牌的一致率分别为50.8%、60.4%和56.7%，而首抓均匀随机基线约为7%。在所有32个已观察扩展上重训后，该架构对当时未发布的The Hobbit系列生成卡牌排名，并在系列可玩前约36小时附完整性加密证明发布；该排名与六名独立专家审阅者的平均一致性大致等于专家彼此间的一致性。

Conclusion: 研究表明即使没有目标系列的历史选秀日志，仅凭公开卡牌文本和结构化特征也能高准确率预测人类选秀行为。同时，研究承诺发布对未来实际结果的事后评估，体现其结论的可检验性。

Abstract: Drafting a new Magic: The Gathering expansion begins before any pick from it has been observed: the complete card list is public, but the draft logs that supervised pick models train on do not yet exist. We study this day-zero regime directly. DraftFM is a discrete-choice policy that scores exactly the cards available in the current pack, conditioned on the drafted pool and the state of the draft. Every card enters as a frozen 775-dimensional function of its public card record, structured features and a fixed text embedding, with no card identities, set identities, or usage statistics anywhere in the model, so an unseen card is scored by the same machinery as a familiar one. A 1.6-million-parameter network fitted on 149 million human picks from 29 expansions predicts held-out picks in three expansions withheld in their entirety, reaching 50.8%, 60.4%, and 56.7% top-1 agreement, where uniform chance at the opening pick is about 7%. Refitted on all 32 observed expansions, the same architecture produced a card ranking for the then-unreleased set The Hobbit, sealed with its complete cryptographic provenance and published roughly 36 hours before the set became draftable on MTG Arena. The sealed ranking agrees with six independent expert reviewers roughly as much as those reviewers agree with one another. Evaluation against realized outcomes is committed to a follow-on note, whatever it shows.

</details>


### [133] [A Two-Stage Time-Aware Transformer for Short-Horizon AECOPD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19578)
*Dongyang Wang,Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段深度学习框架，直接利用家用呼吸机最近七天的原始压力与流量波形：第一阶段分类模型识别严重AECOPD高风险患者，第二阶段回归模型预测事件发生前剩余的天数，在风险分类与时间-事件估计上均优于传统基线模型（分类F1=0.91，回归RMSE=1.00天、R²=0.76）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD病情可迅速恶化，及时预测是临床优先事项。现有机器学习方法依赖间歇性采集的临床变量，存在延迟，难以用于居家监测；而基于呼吸机的现有方法要么将波形压缩为手工特征，要么只做二元风险分类，无法解决事件发生时机的问题。

Method: 构建一个两阶段框架：第一阶段为分类模型，直接在最近七天家用呼吸机使用的原始压力和流量波形上识别高风险患者；第二阶段为回归模型，估计距离事件发生还剩多少天。方法直接作用于原始波形，无需手工特征提取。

Result: 实验表明两阶段模型在风险分类和时间-事件估计上均优于传统基线：选定的第一阶段分类器达到F1=0.91，第二阶段回归模型达到RMSE=1.00天和R²=0.76。

Conclusion: 该方法能在严重恶化发生前为临床医生同时提供早期预警和可操作的前置时间，相比依赖离散临床变量的方法更适用于低延迟的居家监测场景。

Abstract: Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) can worsen rapidly, making timely prediction a clinical priority. Most existing machine learning approaches rely on episodically collected clinical variables, introducing delays that limit their practical utility in home monitoring settings. Home ventilators offer a lower-latency alternative, producing a near-continuous record of respiratory status during daily use. However existing ventilator-based approaches either compress the waveform into handcrafted features or focus primarily on binary risk classification, leaving the timing of an impending event unresolved. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework that operates directly on raw pressure and flow waveforms from the most recent seven days of home ventilator use. The first-stage classification model identifies patients at high risk of a severe exacerbation. The second-stage regression model then estimates how many days remain before the event occurs. Our experimental results demonstrate that the two-stage model outperforms traditional baseline models on both risk classification and time-to-event estimation, with our selected Stage 1 classifier achieving F1 = 0.91 and our Stage 2 regression model achieving RMSE = 1.00 days and R^2 = 0.76, giving clinicians both an early warning and actionable lead time before a severe exacerbation occurs.

</details>


### [134] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何与复几何视角研究复数参数化神经网络的损失景观，指出其下降路径在交叉熵下可通过Wirtinger Hessian诱导出Kähler信息度量，并采用以逆度量缩放损失梯度的自然梯度下降，使路径保持在全纯切丛中。核心关注Calabi-Yau信息流形：在非紧致、带全局势能的设定下，Kähler形式与无处消失的楔积全纯形式（至多差常数）之间满足常行列式条件；在固定行列式下，度量近似低秩（容许特征值误差）会引发爆炸效应。文章进一步揭示负截面曲率会破坏损失景观，并将负Ricci曲率与深度学习理论中的初始化渐近和神经网络保证失效模式联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望借助信息论流形视角和经典优化保证来分析复数参数化网络的损失景观，同时引入诸如Dolbeault渐近之类的复几何工具，以弥补实值深度学习中关于参数化与曲率结构理解的不足。

Method: 采用几何解析的分析框架：以交叉熵下的Wirtinger Hessian构造Kähler信息度量；定义自然梯度下降更新规则（损失微分乘以逆度量）以保持下降路径位于全纯切丛；在非紧致Calabi-Yau度量设定下不依赖Calabi猜想而直接由几何定义全局势能，通过楔积全纯形式与Kähler形式的关系建立常行列式条件；并利用截面曲率与Ricci曲率（负定义）刻画损失景观的失效机制。

Result: 研究发现：常行列式约束下，当度量在特征值容差内近似低秩时会出现爆炸（blow-up）效应；负的截面曲率会颠覆损失景观的良态性，且与负定Ricci曲率相互关联；这些现象与深度学习中初始化渐近以及神经网络的保证在消失和负Ricci曲率下的失效模式相联系。

Conclusion: 论证主要在本论文的几何解析模态中展开，但作者将结论锚定于深度学习理论根基（如初始化渐近），指出负Ricci曲率会导致神经网络理论保证失效，从而为复杂几何景观下的优化与泛化分析提供新的视角。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [135] [Unregularized Convergence of Single-Loop, Entropy-Regularized Natural Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19587)
*Zhiqiang Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了一种单循环熵正则化Natural Actor-Critic算法（在兼容线性函数逼近下），利用非中心化的critic实现稳定的tracking，并依靠正最小动作间隙（Minimal Action Gap）引入指数平移机制，将正则化目标的最优性间隙映射到非正则化目标，从而在Stochastic与Deterministic两种机制下分别获得~O(T_total^{-1})与~O(T_total^{-2/3})（平均迭代）/O~(T_total^{-1/3})（末次迭代）的加速非正则化收敛速率；在表格设定下更达到~O(T_total^{-2/3})，超越无正动作间隙时O(T_total^{-1/2})的最坏情形统计界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵正则化虽被广泛用于稳定和加速Natural Policy Gradient方法，但其能否为非正则化目标带来更快收敛速率尚未被充分探索；现有分析大多依赖双循环架构并假设线性熵罚，与实践中广泛使用的单循环算法存在理论与实际的差距。

Method: 提出并分析单循环熵正则化Natural Actor-Critic算法，采用兼容线性函数逼近与未中心化的critic（保证策略趋向确定性、Fisher信息矩阵退化时tracking仍稳定）；针对随机机制将耦合的actor-critic更新融合为联合Lyapunov递推，针对确定机制转用Policy Mirror Descent框架以规避欧氏几何退化；利用非正则化MDP的正最小动作间隙设计指数平移机制，将正则化间隙映射到非正则化间隙（仅含指数衰减尾项），并固定温度进行调参。

Result: 通过固定温度调参，算法在随机机制下达到~O(T_total^{-1})的非正则化加速收敛速率（含逼近误差项）；在确定机制下平均迭代达到~O(T_total^{-2/3})、末次迭代达到~O(T_total^{-1/3})；在表格设定下平均迭代速率达~O(T_total^{-2/3})，优于无正动作间隙时O(T_total^{-1/2})的最坏情形统计下界。

Conclusion: 本文通过正最小动作间隙与指数平移机制证明了单循环熵正则化Natural Actor-Critic在函数逼近与表格设定下均可加速非正则化目标的收敛，弥合了熵正则化理论（双循环、线性罚）与实践（单循环）之间的鸿沟，并展示了利用动作间隙可突破无间隙情形下的统计速率壁垒。

Abstract: While entropy regularization is widely used to stabilize and accelerate Natural Policy Gradient methods, its ability to yield faster convergence rates for the unregularized objective remains underexplored. Existing analyses often rely on double-loop architectures and invoke a linear entropy penalty. To bridge the gap between theory and practice, we analyze a single-loop, entropy-regularized Natural Actor-Critic algorithm under compatible linear function approximation. By training an uncentered critic, our critic tracking can remain stable even as the training policy approaches determinism and the Fisher information matrix degenerates. We focus on two primary regimes for the optimization landscape: a Stochastic Regime, where we fuse coupled actor-critic updates into a joint Lyapunov recurrence, and a Deterministic Regime, where we pivot to a Policy Mirror Descent framework to circumvent the collapse of Euclidean geometry. By exploiting a positive Minimal Action Gap in the unregularized Markov decision process, we introduce an Exponential Translation mechanism that maps the regularized gap to the unregularized one up to an exponentially decaying tail. By tuning the fixed temperature, our algorithm achieves accelerated unregularized convergence rates, up to approximation-error terms: $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1})$ in the Stochastic Regime, and $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$ for the average iterate alongside $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1/3})$ for the last iterate in the Deterministic Regime. Here, $T_{total}$ denotes the total number of stochastic critic updates (or Monte Carlo rollouts). Furthermore, in the tabular setting, our positive-action-gap analysis yields a $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$ average-iterate rate, surpassing the $\mathcal{O}(T_{total}^{-1/2})$ worst-case statistical barrier that applies without a positive action margin.

</details>


### [136] [Complementary, Not Cumulative: Interaction Effects in Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Navier-Stokes Vortex Shedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19632)
*Devesh Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对DFG/Schafer-Turek非定常圆柱绕流基准问题，系统研究PINN改进技巧的可组合性：单独使用时几乎所有技巧都不优于原始基线，但将SIREN周期激活函数与因果加权组合可解锁新区间，相对OpenFOAM参考解的平均L2误差达4.1%；叠加更多技巧反而导致性能灾难性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN改进技巧通常逐个独立验证，尚不清楚这些技巧能否组合使用并相互增益，因此需要系统研究技巧间的相互作用。

Method: 在DFG/Schafer-Turek非定常圆柱绕流基准上进行深入实验，逐一验证各技巧的独立效果，并测试多技巧组合的效果，以OpenFOAM解为参考。

Result: 单独使用时几乎所有技巧均不优于基线；仅SIREN与因果加权组合能够解锁此前无法达到的求解区间，将速度与压力场重建精度提升至4.1%平均相对L2误差；额外叠加技巧则引发灾难性性能退化。

Conclusion: 单独有效的PINN干预可能发生非线性交互，说明技巧并非可随意叠加，“更复杂的训练配方”并不必然更好，组合应谨慎选择。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) embed governing partial differential equations directly into the training loss, offering a promising alternative to costly CFD solvers for unsteady flows. Yet the growing list of techniques proposed to improve PINN training is typically validated one at a time, leaving open whether these techniques actually compose. We study this question in depth on the DFG/Schafer-Turek unsteady cylinder wake benchmark. In isolation, nearly every technique performs no better than an untreated baseline. However, combining periodic (SIREN) activations with causal weighting unlocks a previously inaccessible regime, reconstructing velocity and pressure fields to within 4.1% average relative L2 error against an OpenFOAM reference solution. Adding further techniques instead causes catastrophic performance degradation, demonstrating that individually effective PINN interventions can interact nonlinearly and that more elaborate training recipes are not necessarily better.

</details>


### [137] [Time-Uniform Self-Normalized Concentration for Discounted Least Squares: Limits and Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19643)
*Yi-Shan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出并纠正了强化学习/赌博机文献中一个被广泛使用的"自归一化加权浓度不等式"（针对非平稳问题的折扣最小二乘估计量）的错误。作者用一个固定参数的一维高斯标量反例证明：该不等式声称的"有界半径"实际上以概率1被穿越。进一步，他们证明在固定折扣与正则化参数下，对δ≤1/2且T/δ足够大时，任何在所述条件次高斯模型类上均匀有效的确定性任意时刻(anytime)边界，在某个时间点必须至少达到Ω(R√log(T/δ))的量级；对非递减边界，该量级在时刻T即被要求。作者定位到证明的错误根源：不同的终止时刻对应不同的高斯混合分布，固定时刻的混合族并不构成单一超鞅，停时论证无法修复这一缺陷。最后，他们证明该加权不等式在每个固定确定性时刻仍然成立，给出有限与无限时域的有效修正，并讨论了对下游分析的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自归一化浓度不等式是赌博机与强化学习分析的标准工具，其中一种广泛使用的加权扩展被宣称可为非平稳问题中的折扣最小二乘估计量提供时间一致（time-uniform）保证。该声称直接支撑许多下游算法与分析的正确性，因此一旦其有效性存疑，需要严格检验并给出修正，以保障相关理论结论的可靠性。

Method: 作者采用（1）一个固定参数的一维高斯标量反例，直接构造以概率1穿越声称有界半径的路径；(2) 对确定性任意时刻边界给出信息论/概率意义上的下界论证，刻画其在时域T内的必经量级；(3) 追溯原证明的数学错误——不同终止时间采用不同的高斯混合分布，导致固定时刻的混合测度无法拼接成一个单一超鞅，停时化论证因此失效；(4) 重新证明加权不等式在固定确定性时刻仍成立，并构造有限时域与无限时域的修正版本。

Result: 反例表明原始加权time-uniform不等式作为任意时刻边界是错的：其在固定参数下以概率1被穿越。下界结果显示，在给定模型类上有效的确定性任意时刻边界的半径必须至少为R√log(T/δ)量级（在T/δ充分大且δ≤1/2时），非递减边界在时间T即达到此量级，这与原不等式声称的"有界半径"（无此对数增长）相矛盾。同时，作者证明该不等式的固定时刻版本仍然有效，并提供有限/无限时域修正边界。

Conclusion: 结论是：该广泛使用的加权自归一化time-uniform不等式在原声称的任意时刻形式下无效，其证明中的超鞅构造存在根本缺陷；正确的使用方式是限定于固定确定性时刻，或采用作者给出的具有√log(T/δ)增长项的修正边界。这提示依赖该不等式的下游强化学习/赌博机分析（如折扣最小二乘估计的置信区间推导）需要相应调整。

Abstract: Self-normalized concentration inequalities are standard tools in bandit and reinforcement-learning analyses. A widely used weighted extension claims an analogous time-uniform guarantee for discounted least-squares estimators in non-stationary problems. A simple scalar Gaussian counterexample with a fixed parameter shows that the claimed bounded radius is crossed with probability one. For fixed discount and regularization parameters, we further show that, when $δ\leq1/2$ and $T/δ$ is sufficiently large, any deterministic anytime boundary valid uniformly over the stated conditionally sub-Gaussian model class must be at least of order $R\sqrt{\log(T/δ)}$ at some time by horizon $T$; for nondecreasing boundaries, this order is required at time $T$. We identify the proof error: different terminal times use different Gaussian mixing distributions, so the fixed-time mixtures do not form one supermartingale, and the stopping-time argument does not repair this failure. Finally, we show that the weighted inequality remains valid at each fixed deterministic time, give valid finite- and infinite-horizon corrections, and discuss consequences for downstream analyses.

</details>


### [138] [DeltaML-Bench: Evaluating Machine Learning Agents on Real-World Research Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19653)
*Josias Moukpe,Priyanka Aryal,Matthew Kenney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeltaML-Bench，一个包含 48 个源于研究论文任务的基准，用于在真实且不完美的开源代码库中评估智能体改进已发表基线的能力。实验评估了 GPT-5 与 Claude Sonnet 4（使用 Modular agent 与基于搜索的 ARG 脚手架），结果显示 ARG 能显著提升任务成功率（GPT-5 在 4×6h 下从 9.4% 提升至 33.9%，在 2×12h 下达 49.0%），并且消除了 Modular 配置中高达 47.9% 的规格欺骗（specification gaming）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅部分还原了自主机器学习实验智能体在真实场景中面临的挑战：它们必须导航异构代码库、修复不完整的训练管道，并在现实计算约束下评估候选改进。作者旨在填补这一空缺，构建更贴近实际部署条件的评测环境。

Method: 构建 DeltaML-Bench，包含 48 个来自研究论文、要求智能体在公开但有缺陷的代码库中改进已发表基线的任务。在两种资源分配（4×6h 与 2×12h）下评估 GPT-5 和 Claude Sonnet 4，分别采用标准 Modular agent 与基于搜索的 ARG 脚手架，并记录成功率与规格欺骗率。

Result: 在 4×6h 分配下，ARG 将 GPT-5 的单次运行成功率从 9.4% 提升至 33.9%；在 2×12h 分配下，GPT-5 ARG 达到 49.0%。Modular 配置的规格欺骗率最高达 47.9%，而所评估的 ARG 配置未观察到任何规格欺骗。

Conclusion: 脚手架设计（scaffolding）与完整性检查（integrity checks）是部署自主 ML 实验智能体的关键考量，合理的搜索式脚手架不仅能显著提升成功率，还能有效抑制投机取巧的作弊行为。

Abstract: Autonomous agents for machine learning experimentation must navigate heterogeneous repositories, repair training pipelines, and evaluate candidate improvements under realistic compute constraints. Existing benchmarks only partially capture these conditions. We introduce DeltaML-Bench, a benchmark comprising 48 tasks sourced from research papers that require agents to improve published baselines within imperfect, open-source repositories. We evaluate GPT-5 and Claude Sonnet 4 with a standard Modular agent and a search-based ARG scaffolding. In the 4 x 6h allocation, ARG raises GPT-5's per-run success rate from 9.4% to 33.9%; in the 2 x 12h allocation, GPT-5 ARG reaches 49.0%. Modular configurations exhibit specification gaming rates as high as 47.9%, while no gaming is observed in the evaluated ARG configurations. These results indicate that scaffolding design and integrity checks are important considerations when deploying agents for autonomous ML experimentation.

</details>


### [139] [Rationally Enriched Chebyshev Trunk Bases for DeepONet Surrogates of High Péclet Entrance Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19658)
*Mingeun Choi,Satish Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种理性增强切比雪夫（REC）主干，用于深度算子网络（DeepONet）求解具有薄边界/壁面层的奇异摄动与高Péclet输运问题。REC主干将切比雪夫多项式字典与基于自适应Antoulas-Anderson（AAA）算法构造的有理字典相结合。在五个独立训练下，相比vanilla DeepONet与纯切比雪夫主干DeepONet，REC主干在标量剖面上优于前者、与后者相当；在摄动参数介于1.00e-4至1.78e-4时相对切比雪夫主干最多降低19.5%的剖面误差；在壁面法向温度与浓度剖面预测上，分别相对两者最多降低60.2%和32.2%的误差，并在Péclet数（或传质Péclet数）为1e2至1e4范围内抑制了近壁人工振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异摄动与高Péclet输运问题的解常含极薄的局部边界/壁面层，标准DeepONet（vanilla）与纯切比雪夫主干难以高效、无振荡地刻画这类陡峭剖面。作者旨在通过显式引入与AAA算法生成的有理函数，增强DeepONet主干对薄层结构的表达能力，从而提升此类问题代理模型的精度与稳定性。

Method: 构建REC（rationally enriched Chebyshev）主干：将切比雪夫多项式字典元素与由自适应Antoulas-Anderson（AAA）算法生成的有理字典元素联合组成主干基函数。在三个基准问题（奇异摄动标量BVP、指定壁温的热入口问题、吸收壁面的浓度入口问题）上，对REC主干DeepONet、vanilla DeepONet和纯切比雪夫主干DeepONet进行五个独立训练运行的对比评估，以扩散/对流比作为奇异摄动参数，并在多个Péclet数下检验壁面法向温度与浓度剖面。

Result: 在保留测试剖面上，REC主干显著优于vanilla DeepONet，并与切比雪夫主干总体相当；其最明显优势出现在摄动参数1.00e-4至1.78e-4区间，此时剖面误差指标相对切比雪夫主干最多下降19.5%。对壁面法向温度与浓度剖面，REC主干相对vanilla和切比雪夫主干分别最多降低60.2%与32.2%的误差，并在Péclet/传质Péclet数1e2至1e4范围内有效抑制人工近壁振荡。

Conclusion: REC主干通过引入AAA生成的有理函数，能够更精准地表示奇异摄动/高Péclet问题中的薄边界层结构，在保持纯切比雪夫主干精度的同时增强鲁棒性，尤其在中低摄动参数区间和壁面法向剖面预测上取得显著误差降低与振荡抑制，是一种适用于薄层输运问题的有效DeepONet代理建模方案。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a rationally enriched Chebyshev (REC) trunk for deep operator network (DeepONet) surrogate models of singularly perturbed and high-Péclet transport problems whose solution profiles are characterized by thin localized boundary or wall layers. The REC trunk combines Chebyshev polynomial dictionary elements with rational dictionary elements constructed using the adaptive Antoulas-Anderson (AAA) algorithm. Over five independent training runs, the resulting REC-trunk DeepONet is evaluated against a vanilla DeepONet and a Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet whose prescribed dictionary consists only of Chebyshev polynomials across three problems whose singular perturbation parameters are diffusion-to-advection ratios: a singularly perturbed scalar boundary-value problem (BVP), the thermal entrance problem with a prescribed wall temperature, and the concentration entrance problem with an absorbing wall. Across the held-out test profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet improves over the vanilla DeepONet and remains comparable to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet in predicting the scalar profile, with its clearest advantage over the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet appearing when the perturbation parameter lies between $1.00\times10^{-4}$ and $1.78\times10^{-4}$, where it reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $19.5\,\%$ relative to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet. In predicting the wall-normal temperature and concentration profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $60.2\,\%$ and $32.2\,\%$ relative to the vanilla and Chebyshev-trunk DeepONets, respectively, while suppressing artificial near-wall oscillations as the Péclet or mass-transfer Péclet number ranges from $10^{2}$ to $10^{4}$.

</details>


### [140] [FleetSieve: Decision-Critical Profiling for SLO-Aware LLM Fleet Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19659)
*Huang Cheng,Scott Zhang,Aubert Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FleetSieve 是一种面向 LLM 服务集群的测量选择方法，它不进行穷举式性能剖析，而是根据每个配置对"资源耦合、满足 SLO 的集群决策"的预期影响来选择测量点，联合建模容量与尾部延迟，对比保守与乐观分配，并在决策差距低于指定容差时提前停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 LLM 服务集群选择张量并行度(TP degree)与副本数(replica count)非常困难：性能在 TP 维度上并不单调，且可行选择会随负载变化。穷举式 profiling 虽能消除不确定性，但会测量大量对最终资源分配毫无影响的配置，造成 GPU 时长的浪费。因此需要一个仅测量"对最终决策有预期影响"的配置的机制。

Method: FleetSieve 的核心方法包括：(1) 依据测量对决策的影响程度来挑选测量配置；(2) 联合建模系统容量与尾部延迟（p99），而非仅看吞吐；(3) 同时评估保守分配与乐观分配两种视角；(4) 当两者之间的剩余决策差距低于设定容差时提前终止测量。评估采用固定 H100 测量网格与 200 个随机揭示顺序，并结合边界重复测量与 BurstGPT 数据验证负载依赖的尾部延迟机制。

Result: 在 31B 参数开放权重模型的固定 H100 网格上，FleetSieve 用 22,200 GPU-秒达到 oracle 聚合决策，比均匀随机 profiling 少 6.9%；在 200 个随机揭示顺序下平均节省 5.4%（95% bootstrap CI: 3.5–7.2%）。固定对比下对 Chat 负载节省 21.5%，但对 Code 负载并非最少 GPU-秒。联合容量与尾部建模避免了选择"46.4 秒完成 p99 违反 30 秒 SLO"的配置；在 16-GPU 分配中，错误的稀疏 profile 决策最多损失 1.93 requests/s 和 12.4 个百分点的 max-min 满足度。

Conclusion: FleetSieve 表明：相对于随机或穷举 profiling，以决策价值为导向的测量选择能以显著更少的 GPU-秒达成与 oracle 一致的资源分配决策；同时，联合建模容量与尾部延迟对于避免违反 SLO 至关重要，且负载依赖的尾部延迟机制经由边界重复与真实流量(BurstGPT)测量得到支持。该方法在部分负载(Code)下不追求最少测量量，体现了决策导向取舍的合理性。

Abstract: Choosing tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and replica counts for an LLM serving fleet is difficult because performance is not monotonic in TP and the feasible choice can change with load. Exhaustive profiling resolves this uncertainty, but measures many configurations that do not affect the final resource allocation. We present FleetSieve, which selects measurements according to their expected effect on a resource-coupled, SLO-aware fleet decision.
